\E[W_{n,32}]=O\left({A_{n}\over n}{b_{1}\dots b_{d}J^{2}\over n}\right)=o(1)
and this yields
W_{n,32}=o_{p}(1)
. The results on
W_{n,31}
and
W_{n,32}
yield

\displaystyle W_{n,3}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle p}}{{\to}}\kappa\left(\widetilde{\psi}_{J}(\bm{z})\over\|\widetilde{\psi}_{J}(\bm{z})\|\right)^{\prime}\left(\int\sigma_{\varepsilon}^{2}(\bm{v})\widetilde{\psi}_{J}(\bm{v})\widetilde{\psi}_{J}(\bm{v})^{\prime}g(\bm{v})d\bm{v}\right)\left(\widetilde{\psi}_{J}(\bm{z})\over\|\widetilde{\psi}_{J}(\bm{z})\|\right)^{\prime}.

(C.29)

For
W_{n,2}
, observe that

\displaystyle\left({2A_{n}\over n^{2}}\right)^{-2}\E_{\cdot|\bm{S}}[W_{n,2}^{2}]

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=1}^{n}k_{J}^{4}(\bm{S}_{i}/A_{n})\eta^{2}(\bm{S}_{i}/A_{n})\sigma_{\varepsilon}^{2}(\bm{S}_{j}/A_{n})

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{i\neq j}^{n}k_{J}^{2}(\bm{S}_{i}/A_{n})k_{J}^{2}(\bm{S}_{j}/A_{n})\bar{K}_{b}(\bm{S}_{i}-\bm{S}_{j})\eta(\bm{S}_{i}/A_{n})\eta(\bm{S}_{j}/A_{n})\sigma_{\varepsilon}^{2}(\bm{S}_{j}/A_{n})

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{i\neq j}^{n}k_{J}(\bm{S}_{i}/A_{n})k_{J}^{3}(\bm{S}_{j}/A_{n})\bar{K}_{b}(\bm{S}_{i}-\bm{S}_{j})\eta(\bm{S}_{i}/A_{n})\eta(\bm{S}_{j}/A_{n})\sigma_{\bm{e}}(\bm{S}_{i}-\bm{S}_{j})\sigma_{\varepsilon}^{2}(\bm{S}_{j}/A_{n})

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{i\neq j\neq\ell}^{n}k_{J}(\bm{S}_{i}/A_{n})k_{J}^{2}(\bm{S}_{j}/A_{n})k_{J}(\bm{S}_{\ell})\bar{K}_{b}(\bm{S}_{i}-\bm{S}_{j})\bar{K}_{b}(\bm{S}_{\ell}-\bm{S}_{j})

\displaystyle\quad\quad\times\eta(\bm{S}_{i}/A_{n})\eta(\bm{S}_{j}/A_{n})\sigma_{\bm{e}}(\bm{S}_{i}-\bm{S}_{\ell})\sigma_{\varepsilon}^{2}(\bm{S}_{j}/A_{n})=:W_{n,21}+W_{n,22}+W_{n,23}+W_{n,24}

where
\E_{\cdot|\bm{S}}[\cdot]
denote the conditional expectation given the
\sigma
-field generated by
\{\bm{S}_{i}\}_{i\geq 1}
. For
W_{n,21}
, we have
\E[W_{n,21}]=O(nJ^{2})
and this yields
\left({2A_{n}\over n^{2}}\right)^{2}\!\E[W_{n,21}]=o(1)
. For
W_{n,22}
, applying similar arguments to show (
C.28
), we have
\E[W_{n,22}]\lesssim n^{2}A_{n}^{-1}b_{1}\dots b_{d}J^{2}
. This yields
\left({2A_{n}\over n^{2}}\right)^{2}\E[W_{n,22}]=o(1)
. For
W_{n,23}
, applying similar arguments in the proof of Theorem
2.2
, we have

\displaystyle\E[W_{n,23}]

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{i\neq j}^{n}\E\left[k_{J}(\bm{S}_{i}/A_{n})k_{J}^{3}(\bm{S}_{j}/A_{n})\eta(\bm{X}_{i}/A_{n})\eta(\bm{X}_{j}/A_{n})\sigma_{\bm{e}}(\bm{X}_{i}-\bm{X}_{j})\sigma_{\varepsilon}^{2}(\bm{X}_{j}/A_{n})\right]

\displaystyle=O\left(n^{2}A_{n}^{-1}J^{2}\right).

This yields
\left({2A_{n}\over n^{2}}\right)^{2}\E[W_{n,23}]=O\left({A_{n}\over n}{J^{2}\over n}\right)=o(1)
.

For
W_{n,24}
, applying similar arguments in the proof of Theorem
2.2
, we have

\displaystyle\E[W_{n,24}]

\displaystyle=n(n-1)(n-2)\int_{R_{0}^{3}}\bar{K}_{b}(A_{n}(\bm{z}_{1}-\bm{z}_{2}))\bar{K}_{b}(A_{n}(\bm{z}_{3}-\bm{z}_{2}))

\displaystyle\quad\times\sigma_{\bm{e}}(A_{n}(\bm{z}_{1}-\bm{z}_{3}))k_{J}(\bm{z}_{1})k_{J}^{2}(\bm{z}_{2})k_{J}(\bm{z}_{3})\eta(\bm{z}_{1})\eta(\bm{z}_{2})\sigma_{\varepsilon}^{2}\left(\bm{z}_{2}\right)

\displaystyle\quad\times A_{n}^{-3}g(\bm{z}_{1})g(\bm{z}_{2})g(\bm{z}_{3})d\bm{z}_{1}d\bm{z}_{2}d\bm{z}_{3}

\displaystyle=n(n-1)(n-2)A_{n}^{-1}\int_{R_{0}}k_{J}^{2}(\bm{z}_{2})\sigma_{\varepsilon}^{2}(\bm{z}_{2})g(\bm{z}_{2})

\displaystyle\quad\left\{\int_{\bar{R}_{n}}\sigma_{\bm{e}}(\bm{v})\left(\int\bar{K}\left({\bm{v}+A_{n}(\bm{z}_{3}-\bm{z}_{2})\over\bm{b}}\right)\bar{K}\left({A_{n}(\bm{z}_{3}-\bm{z}_{2})\over\bm{b}}\right)\right.\right.

\displaystyle\left.\left.\quad\times k_{J}\!\!\left(\!\bm{z}_{3}+{\bm{v}\over A_{n}}\!\right)\!k_{J}(\bm{z}_{3})\eta\!\left(\!\bm{z}_{3}\!+{\bm{v}\over A_{n}}\!\right)\!\eta\left(\bm{z}_{3}\!\right)\!g\!\left(\bm{z}_{3}\!+{\bm{v}\over A_{n}}\right)\!g\left(\bm{z}_{3}\right)d\bm{z}_{3}\!\right)\!d\bm{v}\!\right\}\!d\bm{z}_{2}

\displaystyle=n(n-1)(n-2)A_{n}^{-2}b_{1}\dots b_{d}\int_{R_{0}}k_{J}^{2}(\bm{z}_{2})\sigma_{\varepsilon}^{2}(\bm{z}_{2})g(\bm{z}_{2})

\displaystyle\quad\left\{\int_{\bar{R}_{n}}\sigma_{\bm{e}}(\bm{v})\left(\int\bar{K}\left({\bm{v}\over\bm{b}}+\bm{w}-{A_{n}\bm{z}_{2}\over\bm{b}}\right)\bar{K}\left(\bm{w}-{A_{n}\bm{z}_{2}\over\bm{b}}\right)\right.\right.

\displaystyle\left.\left.\quad\times k_{J}\!\!\left(\!{\bm{w}\circ\bm{b}\over A_{n}}+{\bm{v}\over A_{n}}\!\right)\!k_{J}\!\left({\bm{w}\!\circ\!\bm{b}\over A_{n}}\right)\!\eta\!\left(\!{\bm{w}\!\circ\!\bm{b}\over A_{n}}+{\bm{v}\over A_{n}}\!\right)\!\eta\!\left({\bm{w}\!\circ\!\bm{b}\over A_{n}}\right)\right.\right.

\displaystyle\left.\left.\quad\times g\!\left({\bm{w}\!\circ\!\bm{b}\over A_{n}}+{\bm{v}\over A_{n}}\right)\!g\!\left({\bm{w}\!\circ\!\bm{b}\over A_{n}}\right)\!d\bm{w}\!\right)\!d\bm{v}\!\right\}\!d\bm{z}_{2}=O\left(n^{3}A_{n}^{-2}b_{1}\dots b_{d}J^{2}\right).

This yields
\left({2A_{n}\over n^{2}}\right)^{2}\E[W_{n,24}]=O\left({b_{1}\dots b_{d}J^{2}\over n}\right)=o(1)
. The results on
W_{n,21}

W_{n,22}
,
W_{n,23}
, and
W_{n,24}
yield

\displaystyle W_{n,2}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle p}}{{\to}}0.

(C.30)

For
W_{n,1}
, applying similar arguments to show (
C.28
), we have

\displaystyle\left({A_{n}\over n^{2}}\right)^{-1}\E[W_{n,1}]

\displaystyle=n\int k_{J}^{2}(\bm{v})\eta^{2}(\bm{v})g(\bm{v})d\bm{v}+n(n-1)A_{n}^{-1}\int_{\bar{R}_{n}}\!\!\!\!\sigma_{\bm{e}}(\bm{v})\bar{K}_{b}(\bm{v})\left(\int\!\!k_{J}\!\!\left(\!\bm{z}_{2}\!+\!{\bm{v}\over A_{n}}\!\right)\!\!k_{J}(\bm{z}_{2})\right.

\displaystyle\left.\quad\times\eta\!\left(\!\bm{z}_{2}\!+\!{\bm{v}\over A_{n}}\!\right)\!\eta(\bm{z}_{2})g\!\left(\!\bm{z}_{2}\!+\!{\bm{v}\over A_{n}}\!\right)g(\bm{z}_{2})d\bm{z}_{2}\!\right)d\bm{v}=:W_{n,11}+W_{n,12}.

For
W_{n,12}
, we have

\displaystyle W_{n,12}

\displaystyle={n(n-1)\over A_{n}}\int_{\bar{R}_{n}}\!\!\!\sigma_{\bm{e}}(\bm{v})\left(\int k_{J}\!\!\left(\!\bm{z}_{2}\!+\!{\bm{v}\over A_{n}}\!\right)\!\!k_{J}(\bm{z}_{2})\eta\!\left(\!\bm{z}_{2}\!+\!{\bm{v}\over A_{n}}\!\right)\!\eta(\bm{z}_{2})g\!\left(\!\bm{z}_{2}\!+\!{\bm{v}\over A_{n}}\!\right)g(\bm{z}_{2})d\bm{z}_{2}\!\right)d\bm{v}

\displaystyle\quad+{n(n-1)\over A_{n}}\int_{\bar{R}_{n}}\!\!\!\sigma_{\bm{e}}(\bm{v})(\bar{K}_{b}(\bm{v})-1)\left(\int k_{J}\!\!\left(\!\bm{z}_{2}\!+\!{\bm{v}\over A_{n}}\!\right)\!\!k_{J}(\bm{z}_{2})\right.

\displaystyle\left.\quad\times\eta\!\left(\!\bm{z}_{2}\!+\!{\bm{v}\over A_{n}}\!\right)\!\eta(\bm{z}_{2})g\!\left(\!\bm{z}_{2}\!+\!{\bm{v}\over A_{n}}\!\right)g(\bm{z}_{2})d\bm{z}_{2}\!\right)d\bm{v}=:W_{n,121}+W_{n,122}.

For
W_{n,121}
, from the proof of Theorem
2.2
, we have

\displaystyle W_{n,121}

\displaystyle={n^{2}\over A_{n}}\left({\widetilde{\psi}_{J}(\bm{z})\over\|\widetilde{\psi}_{J}(\bm{z})\|}\right)^{\prime}\left(\int\eta^{2}(\bm{v})\widetilde{\psi}_{J}(\bm{v})\widetilde{\psi}_{J}(\bm{v})^{\prime}g^{2}(\bm{v})d\bm{v}\right)\left(\int\sigma_{\bm{e}}(\bm{x})d\bm{x}\right)\left({\widetilde{\psi}_{J}(\bm{z})\over\|\widetilde{\psi}_{J}(\bm{z})\|}\right)(1+o(1)).

For
W_{n,122}
, observe that for any
M>0
,

\displaystyle W_{n,122}

\displaystyle=n(n-1)A_{n}^{-1}\int_{\bar{R}_{n}\cap\{\|\bm{v}\|\leq M\}}\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\sigma_{\bm{e}}(\bm{v})(\bar{K}_{b}(\bm{v})-1)\left(\int\!\!k_{J}\!\!\left(\!\bm{z}_{2}\!+\!{\bm{v}\over A_{n}}\!\right)\!\!k_{J}(\bm{z}_{2})\eta\!\left(\!\bm{z}_{2}\!+\!{\bm{v}\over A_{n}}\!\right)\!\eta(\bm{z}_{2})\right.

\displaystyle\left.\quad\times g\!\left(\!\bm{z}_{2}\!+\!{\bm{v}\over A_{n}}\!\right)g(\bm{z}_{2})d\bm{z}_{2}\!\right)d\bm{v}

\displaystyle+n(n-1)A_{n}^{-1}\int_{\bar{R}_{n}\cap\{\|\bm{v}\|>M\}}\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\sigma_{\bm{e}}(\bm{v})(\bar{K}_{b}(\bm{v})-1)\left(\int\!\!k_{J}\!\!\left(\!\bm{z}_{2}\!+\!{\bm{v}\over A_{n}}\!\right)\!\!k_{J}(\bm{z}_{2})\eta\!\left(\!\bm{z}_{2}\!+\!{\bm{v}\over A_{n}}\!\right)\!\eta(\bm{z}_{2})\right.

\displaystyle\left.\quad\times g\!\left(\!\bm{z}_{2}\!+\!{\bm{v}\over A_{n}}\!\right)g(\bm{z}_{2})d\bm{z}_{2}\!\right)d\bm{v}=:W_{n,1221}+W_{n,1222}.

Observe that

\displaystyle|W_{n,1221}|

\displaystyle\lesssim n^{2}A_{n}^{-1}{M\over\min_{1\leq j\leq d}b_{j}},\ |W_{n,1222}|\lesssim n^{2}A_{n}^{-1}\int_{\|\bm{v}\|>M}|\sigma_{\bm{e}}(\bm{v})|d\bm{v}.

Then by taking

, we have

\displaystyle W_{n,1221}

\displaystyle=O\left(n^{2}A_{n}^{-1}(\min_{1\leq j\leq d}b_{j})^{-1/2}\right),\ W_{n,1222}=o\left(n^{2}A_{n}^{-1}\right).

The results on
W_{n,121}
,
W_{n,1221}
, and
W_{n,1222}
yield

W_{n,12}={n^{2}\over A_{n}}\left({\widetilde{\psi}_{J}(\bm{z})\over\|\widetilde{\psi}_{J}(\bm{z})\|}\right)^{\prime}\left(\int\eta^{2}(\bm{v})\widetilde{\psi}_{J}(\bm{v})\widetilde{\psi}_{J}(\bm{v})^{\prime}g^{2}(\bm{v})d\bm{v}\right)\left(\int\sigma_{\bm{e}}(\bm{x})d\bm{x}\right)\left({\widetilde{\psi}_{J}(\bm{z})\over\|\widetilde{\psi}_{J}(\bm{z})\|}\right)(1+o(1)).

This yields

\displaystyle\E[W_{n,1}]

\displaystyle=\left({\widetilde{\psi}_{J}(\bm{z})\over\|\widetilde{\psi}_{J}(\bm{z})\|}\right)^{\prime}\left\{\kappa\int\eta^{2}(\bm{v})\widetilde{\psi}_{J}(\bm{v})\widetilde{\psi}_{J}(\bm{v})^{\prime}g(\bm{v})d\bm{v}\right.

\displaystyle\left.\quad+\left(\int\eta^{2}(\bm{v})\widetilde{\psi}_{J}(\bm{v})\widetilde{\psi}_{J}(\bm{v})^{\prime}g^{2}(\bm{v})d\bm{v}\right)\left(\int\sigma_{\bm{e}}(\bm{x})d\bm{x}\right)\right\}\left({\widetilde{\psi}_{J}(\bm{z})\over\|\widetilde{\psi}_{J}(\bm{z})\|}\right)(1+o(1)).

(C.31)

Combining (
C.29
), (
C.30
), (
C.3
) and the results in the proof of Theorem
2.2
, we obtain (
C.27
)
and this yields the desired result.

Appendix D
Proof for Section
3

D.1.
Proof of Proposition
3.1

Note that

\displaystyle{\widetilde{\psi}_{J}(\bm{z})^{\prime}\widetilde{\Psi}_{J,n}^{\prime}e_{n}\over n}={1\over n}\!\sum_{i=1}^{n}\widetilde{\psi}_{J}(z)^{\prime}\widetilde{\psi}_{J}\!\left({\bm{S}_{i}\over A_{n}}\right)\eta\!\left({\bm{S}_{i}\over A_{n}}\right)\!e_{1,m_{n}}\!(\bm{S}_{i})+{1\over n}\!\sum_{i=1}^{n}\widetilde{\psi}_{J}(z)^{\prime}\widetilde{\psi}_{J}\!\left({\bm{S}_{i}\over A_{n}}\right)\eta\!\left({\bm{S}_{i}\over A_{n}}\right)e_{2,m_{n}}\!(\bm{S}_{i}).

To verify the asymptotic negligibility of the random field
\bm{e}_{2,m_{n}}
, it suffices to show that

\displaystyle\max_{1\leq i\leq n}|e_{2,m_{n}}(\bm{S}_{i})|=O_{p}\left(\exp\left(-{r_{1}\over 8}n^{{\mathfrak{c}_{0}\mathfrak{c}_{1}\mathfrak{c}_{2}\over 2d}}\right)\right),\ n\to\infty.

(D.1)

Indeed, (
D.1
) yields that

\displaystyle\sup_{\bm{z}\in R_{0}}\left|{1\over n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\widetilde{\psi}_{J}(z)^{\prime}\widetilde{\psi}_{J}\left({\bm{S}_{i}\over A_{n}}\right)\eta\left({\bm{S}_{i}\over A_{n}}\right)e_{2,m_{n}}(\bm{S}_{i})\right|

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{\bm{z}\in R_{0}}\|\widetilde{\psi}_{J}(\bm{z})\|{1\over n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left\|\widetilde{\psi}_{J}\left({\bm{S}_{i}\over A_{n}}\right)\right\|\left|\eta\left({\bm{S}_{i}\over A_{n}}\right)\right||e_{2,m_{n}}(\bm{S}_{i})|

\displaystyle\lesssim(\zeta_{J}\lambda_{J})^{2}\max_{1\leq i\leq n}|e_{2,m_{n}}(\bm{S}_{i})|\lesssim\exp\left(-{r_{1}\over 16}n^{{\mathfrak{c}_{0}\mathfrak{c}_{1}\mathfrak{c}_{2}\over 2d}}\right),

which implies that
\bm{e}_{2,m_{n}}
is asymptotically negligible. Hence we can replace
\bm{e}
with
\bm{e}_{1,m_{n}}
in the results in Section
2
.

Now we show (
D.1
). Note that under Condition (a), we have
E[|e(\bm{0})|^{9}]<\infty
since
\bm{e}
is Gaussian. Under Condition (b), we also have
E[|L([0,1]^{d})|^{9}]<\infty
since
\int_{|x|>1}|x|^{9}\nu_{0}(x)dx<\infty
(cf. Theorem 25.3 in
Sato, 1999
). Define
\sigma_{\bm{e}_{1,m_{n}}}(\bm{x})=E[e_{1,m_{n}}(\bm{0})e_{1,m_{n}}(\bm{x})]
.
Then we have that

\displaystyle E[|e_{1,m_{n}}(\bm{0})|^{9}]

\displaystyle\leq E[|e(\bm{0})|^{9}]\lesssim\int e^{-9r_{1}\|\bm{u}\|}d\bm{u}<\infty,

\displaystyle|\sigma_{\bm{e}_{1,m_{n}}}\!(\bm{x})|

\displaystyle\lesssim\!|E[e(\bm{0})e(\bm{x})]|\!\lesssim\!\int\!\!\!e^{-r_{1}\|\bm{u}\|}e^{-r_{1}\|\bm{x}-\bm{u}\|}d\bm{u}\leq\!\int\!\!\!e^{-r_{1}\|\bm{u}\|}e^{-{r_{1}\over 2}(\|\bm{x}\|-\|\bm{u}\|)}d\bm{u}\lesssim e^{-{r_{1}\over 2}\|\bm{x}\|}.

The latter implies that
\int|\sigma_{\bm{e}_{1,m_{n}}}(\bm{v})|d\bm{v}<\infty
. Likewise,

\displaystyle E[(e_{2,m_{n}}(\bm{0}))^{4}]

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}\!\!\!e^{-4r_{1}\|\bm{u}\|}\left(1-\mathfrak{t}\left(\|\bm{u}\|:m_{n}\right)\right)^{4}d\bm{u}\lesssim\int_{\|\bm{u}\|\geq m_{n}/4}\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!e^{-4r_{1}\|\bm{u}\|}\left|1+{4\over m_{n}}\left(\|\bm{u}\|-{m_{n}\over 2}\right)\right|^{4}d\bm{u}

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{\|\bm{u}\|\geq m_{n}/4}\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!e^{-4r_{1}\|\bm{u}\|}\left|1+{4\|\bm{u}\|\over m_{n}}\right|^{4}d\bm{u}\leq 2^{q-1}\int_{\|\bm{u}\|\geq m_{n}/4}\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!e^{-4r_{1}\|\bm{u}\|}\left(1+{4^{4}\|\bm{u}\|^{4}\over m_{n}^{4}}\right)d\bm{u}

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{m_{n}/4}^{\infty}e^{-4r_{1}t}\left(1+{4^{4}t^{4}\over m_{n}^{4}}\right)t^{d-1}dt\lesssim m_{n}^{d-1}e^{-r_{1}m_{n}}.

By Markov’s inequality and Lemma 2.2.2 in
van der Vaart and Wellner, 1996
, we have

\displaystyle\Prob\left(\max_{1\leq i\leq n}\left|e_{2,m_{n}}(\bm{S}_{i})\right|>\varrho\right)

\displaystyle\leq\varrho^{-1}\E\!\left[\max_{1\leq i\leq n}\left|e_{2,m_{n}}(\bm{S}_{i})\right|\right]\leq\varrho^{-1}n^{1/4}\max_{1\leq i\leq n}\left(\!\E\left[\left|e_{2,m_{n}}(\bm{0})\right|^{4}\right]\right)^{1/4}

\displaystyle\lesssim\varrho^{-1}n^{1/4}m_{n}^{(d-1)/4}e^{-r_{1}m_{n}/4}.

Letting
\varrho=\exp\left(-{r_{1}\over 8}n^{{\mathfrak{c}_{0}\mathfrak{c}_{1}\mathfrak{c}_{2}\over 2d}}\right)
, we have (
D.1
).

Next, we verify that the random field
\bm{e}_{1,m_{n}}
satisfies our regularity conditions. Let
\alpha_{\bm{e}_{1}}(a;b)
be the
\alpha
-mixing coefficients of
\bm{e}_{1,m_{n}}
. Note that
\alpha_{\bm{e}_{1}}(a;b)\leq\alpha(a;b)
. Since
\bm{e}_{1,m_{n}}
is
m_{n}
-dependent, we have
\alpha_{2}(\underline{A}_{n2})=0
, which yields

\displaystyle A_{n}(A_{n}^{(1)})^{-1}\alpha_{1}(\underline{A}_{n2})\alpha_{2}(A_{n})=0,

(D.2)

\displaystyle A_{n}^{(1)}(\zeta_{J}\lambda_{J})^{2}\left(\alpha_{1}^{1-2/q}(\underline{A}_{n2})+\sum_{k=\underline{A}_{n1}}^{\infty}k^{d-1}\alpha_{1}^{1-2/q}(k)\right)\alpha_{2}^{1-2/q}(A_{n}^{(1)})=0.

(D.3)

Moreover,

\displaystyle A_{n}^{-1}(\bar{A}_{n1})^{d}(\zeta_{J}\lambda_{J})^{4}\sum_{k=1}^{\overline{A}_{n1}}k^{2d-1}\alpha_{1}^{1-4/q}(k)

\displaystyle\lesssim A_{n}^{-1}(\bar{A}_{n1})^{d}J^{2}\sum_{k=1}^{m_{n}}k^{2d-1}\leq A_{n}^{-1}(\bar{A}_{n1})^{d}J^{2}m_{n}^{2d}

\displaystyle\lesssim n^{-\mathfrak{c}_{0}\left\{1-\mathfrak{c}_{1}(1+\mathfrak{c}_{2})\right\}}J^{2}=o(1).

(D.4)

\displaystyle\left({(\overline{A}_{n1})^{d-1}\overline{A}_{n2}\over A_{n}^{(1)}}+{A_{n}^{(1)}\over A_{n}}\left({\overline{A}_{n}\over\underline{A}_{n1}}\right)^{d-1}\right)(\zeta_{J}\lambda_{J})^{2}\sum_{k=1}^{\overline{A}_{n1}}k^{d-1}\alpha_{1}^{1-2/q}(k)

\displaystyle\lesssim\left\{n^{{\mathfrak{c}_{0}\mathfrak{c}_{1}\mathfrak{c}_{2}\over d}-{\mathfrak{c}_{0}\mathfrak{c}_{1}\over d}}+n^{{\mathfrak{c}_{0}\mathfrak{c}_{1}\over d}-{\mathfrak{c}_{0}\over d}}\right\}Jm_{n}^{d}

\displaystyle\lesssim n^{-\mathfrak{c}_{0}\mathfrak{c}_{1}\{{(1-\mathfrak{c}_{2})\over d}-{\mathfrak{c}_{2}\over 2}\}}J=o(1).

(D.5)

Then Assumption
2.6
holds from (
D.2
), (
D.3
), (
D.4
), and (
D.5
). We can also verify Assumption
2.4
and
A_{n}^{1/2}J^{-r/d}=o(1)
, which implies
A_{n}^{1/2}(\min_{1\leq\ell\leq L}\|\widetilde{\psi}_{J}(\bm{z}_{\ell})\|)^{-1}\inf_{m\in\Psi_{J}}\|m_{0}-m\|_{\infty}=o(1)
. Therefore, we obtain the desired result.

Appendix E
Proofs for Section
B

E.1.
Proof of Proposition
B.1

Define
\check{B}_{J}^{(w)}=\E[b_{J}^{(w)}(\bm{S}_{1}/A_{n},\bm{X}(\bm{S}_{1}))b_{J}^{(w)}(\bm{S}_{1}/A_{n},\bm{X}(\bm{S}_{1}))^{\prime}]
and

\bar{\mathfrak{m}}(\bm{z},\bm{x})=\widetilde{b}_{J}^{(w)}(\bm{z},\bm{x})^{\prime}(\widetilde{B}_{J,n}^{(w)^{\prime}}\widetilde{B}_{J,n}^{(w)}/n+\varsigma_{J,n}(\check{B}_{J}^{(w)})^{-1})^{-1}\widetilde{B}_{J,n}^{(w)^{\prime}}\mathfrak{M}_{0}/n.

Note that

\displaystyle\widehat{\mathfrak{m}}(\bm{z},\bm{x})-\widetilde{\mathfrak{m}}(\bm{z},\bm{x})

\displaystyle=\widetilde{b}_{J}^{(w)}(\bm{z},\bm{x})^{\prime}(\widetilde{B}_{J,n}^{(w)^{\prime}}\widetilde{B}_{J,n}^{(w)}/n+\varsigma_{J,n}(\check{B}_{J}^{(w)})^{-1})^{-1}\widetilde{B}_{J,n}^{(w)^{\prime}}\nu_{n}/n

\displaystyle\quad+\widetilde{b}_{J}^{(w)}\!(\bm{z},\bm{x})^{\prime}\!\left\{(\widetilde{B}_{J,n}^{(w)^{\prime}}\widetilde{B}_{J,n}^{(w)}/n\!+\!\varsigma_{J,n}(\check{B}_{J}^{(w)})^{-1})^{-1}\!-(\widetilde{B}_{J,n}^{(w)^{\prime}}\widetilde{B}_{J,n}^{(w)}/n)^{-}\right\}\!\widetilde{B}_{J,n}^{(w)^{\prime}}\mathfrak{M}_{0}/n,

where
\nu_{n}=(\nu_{n,1},\dots,\nu_{n,n})^{\prime}
. Let
\mathcal{U}_{n}
be the smallest subset of
R_{0}
such that for each
\bm{z}\in R_{0}
there exists a
\bm{z}_{n}\in R_{0}
with
\|\bm{z}-\bm{z}_{n}\|\leq\eta_{6}n^{-\eta_{5}}
for some
\eta_{5},\eta_{6}>0
. Applying Lemma
F.3
and almost the same argument in (Step 1) of the proof of Proposition
2.1
, we have

\displaystyle\Prob\left(\|\widehat{\mathfrak{m}}-\bar{\mathfrak{m}}\|_{\infty}\geq 8C\zeta_{J,n}\lambda_{J,n}\sqrt{{\log n\over n}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\!\Prob\left(\!\max_{(\bm{z}_{n},\bm{x}_{n})\in\mathcal{U}_{n}\times D_{n}}\!\left|\widetilde{b}_{J}^{(w)}\!(\bm{z}_{n},\bm{x}_{n})^{\prime}\!\left\{\!\left(\!{\widetilde{B}_{J,n}^{(w)^{\prime}}\widetilde{B}_{J,n}^{(w)}\over n}\!+\varsigma_{J,n}(\check{B}_{J}^{(w)})^{-1}\!\!\right)^{-1}\!\!\!\!\!\!-\!I_{J}\!\right\}\!{\widetilde{B}_{J,n}^{(w)}\nu_{n}\over n}\right|\!\geq\!2C\zeta_{J,n}\lambda_{J,n}\!\sqrt{{\log n\over n}}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+\Prob\left(\max_{(\bm{z}_{n},\bm{x}_{n})\in\mathcal{U}_{n}\times D_{n}}\left|\widetilde{b}_{J}^{(w)}(\bm{z}_{n},\bm{x}_{n})^{\prime}\widetilde{B}_{J,n}^{(w)^{\prime}}\nu_{n}/n\right|\geq 2C\zeta_{J,n}\lambda_{J,n}\sqrt{{\log n\over n}}\right)+o(1)

\displaystyle=:\Upsilon_{n,1}+\Upsilon_{n,2}+o(1).

Applying almost the same argument to control
P_{n,1}
and
P_{n,2}
in the proof of Proposition
2.1
, we have
\Upsilon_{n,1}=o(1)
and
\Upsilon_{n,2}=o(1)
. Further, let
\mathcal{V}_{n}
be the smallest subset of
R_{0}
such that for each
\bm{z}\in R_{0}
there exists a
\bm{z}_{n}\in R_{0}
with
\|\bm{z}-\bm{z}_{n}\|\leq\eta_{8}n^{-\eta_{7}}
for some
\eta_{7},\eta_{8}>0
. Applying Lemma
F.3
and almost the same argument in (Step 4) of the proof of Proposition
2.1
, we have

\displaystyle\Prob\left(\|\bar{\mathfrak{m}}-\widetilde{\mathfrak{m}}\|_{\infty}\geq 4C\varsigma_{J,n}\zeta_{J,n}^{2}\|\mathfrak{m}_{0}\|_{w,\infty}\right)

\displaystyle=\Prob\left(\max_{\bm{z}_{n}\in\mathcal{V}_{n}}|\bar{\mathfrak{m}}(\bm{z}_{n})-\widetilde{\mathfrak{m}}(\bm{z}_{n})|\geq 2C\varsigma_{J,n}\zeta_{J,n}^{2}\|\mathfrak{m}_{0}\|_{w,\infty}\right)+o(1)

\displaystyle=:\Upsilon_{n,3}+o(1).

Applying almost the same argument to control
P_{n,3}
in the proof of Proposition
2.1
, we have
\Upsilon_{n,3}=o(1)
.

B.1

Analogous to the proof of Corollary
2.1
.

2.1

E.6.
Proof of Theorem
B.2

Let
(\bm{z},\bm{x})=(\bm{z}_{1},\bm{x}_{1})
. We prove

\displaystyle{\sqrt{n}(\widehat{\mathfrak{m}}(\bm{z},\bm{x})-\mathfrak{m}_{0}(\bm{z},\bm{x}))\over\sqrt{V_{J}(\bm{z},\bm{x})}}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle d}}{{\to}}N(0,1).

For the multivariate case (i.e.,
L\geq 2
), the desired result follows from the Camér-Wold device.

Note that

\displaystyle{\sqrt{n}(\widehat{\mathfrak{m}}(\bm{z},\bm{x})-\mathfrak{m}_{0}(\bm{z},\bm{x}))\over\sqrt{V_{J}(\bm{z},\bm{x})}}

\displaystyle\quad={\sqrt{n}(\widehat{\mathfrak{m}}(\bm{z},\bm{x})-\bar{\mathfrak{m}}(\bm{z},\bm{x}))\over\sqrt{V_{J}(\bm{z},\bm{x})}}+{\sqrt{n}(\bar{\mathfrak{m}}(\bm{z},\bm{x})-\widetilde{\mathfrak{m}}(\bm{z},\bm{x}))\over\sqrt{V_{J}(\bm{z},\bm{x})}}+{\sqrt{n}(\widetilde{\mathfrak{m}}(\bm{z},\bm{x})-\mathfrak{m}_{0}(\bm{z},\bm{x}))\over\sqrt{V_{J}(\bm{z},\bm{x})}}.

Observe that

\displaystyle V_{J}(\bm{z},\bm{x})

\displaystyle\sim\widetilde{b}_{J}^{(w)}(\bm{z},\bm{x})^{\prime}\E\left[\widetilde{b}_{J}^{(w)}\left({\bm{S}_{1}\over A_{n}},\bm{X}(\bm{S}_{1})\right)\widetilde{b}_{J}^{(w)}\left({\bm{S}_{1}\over A_{n}},\bm{X}(\bm{S}_{1})\right)^{\prime}\right]\widetilde{b}_{J}^{(w)}(\bm{z},\bm{x})

\displaystyle=\widetilde{b}_{J}^{(w)}(\bm{z},\bm{x})^{\prime}\widetilde{b}_{J}^{(w)}(\bm{z},\bm{x})=\|\widetilde{b}_{J}^{(w)}(\bm{z},\bm{x})\|^{2}.

(E.1)

Condition (b) yields that

\left|{\sqrt{n}(\widetilde{\mathfrak{m}}(\bm{z},\bm{x})-\mathfrak{m}_{0}(\bm{z},\bm{x}))\over\sqrt{V_{J}(\bm{z},\bm{x})}}\right|=O_{p}\left(\sqrt{n}\|\widetilde{b}_{J}^{(w)}(\bm{z},\bm{x})\|^{-1}\inf_{\mathfrak{m}\in B_{J}^{(w)}}\|\mathfrak{m}_{0}-\mathfrak{m}\|_{\infty}\right)=o_{p}(1).

Condition (c) yields that

\left|{\sqrt{n}(\bar{\mathfrak{m}}(\bm{z},\bm{x})-\widetilde{\mathfrak{m}}(\bm{z},\bm{x}))\over\sqrt{V_{J}(\bm{z},\bm{x})}}\right|=O_{p}\left(\|\widetilde{b}_{J}^{(w)}(\bm{z},\bm{x})\|^{-1}\varsigma_{J,n}\zeta_{J,n}^{2}\|\mathfrak{m}_{0}\|_{w,\infty}\sqrt{n}\right)=o_{p}(1).

Then it is sufficient to show that

\displaystyle{\sqrt{n}(\widehat{\mathfrak{m}}(\bm{z},\bm{x})-\bar{\mathfrak{m}}(\bm{z},\bm{x}))\over\sqrt{V_{J}(\bm{z},\bm{x})}}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle d}}{{\to}}N(0,1).

(E.2)

Now we show (
E.2
). Recall that

\widehat{\mathfrak{m}}(\bm{z},\bm{x})-\bar{\mathfrak{m}}(\bm{z},\bm{x})=\widetilde{b}_{J}^{(w)}(\bm{z},\bm{x})^{\prime}(\widetilde{B}_{J,n}^{(w)^{\prime}}\widetilde{B}_{J,n}^{(w)}/n+\varsigma_{J,n}(\check{B}_{J}^{(w)})^{-1})^{-1}\widetilde{B}_{J,n}^{(w)^{\prime}}\nu_{n}/n.

Decompose

\displaystyle{\sqrt{n}(\widehat{\mathfrak{m}}(\bm{z},\bm{x})-\bar{\mathfrak{m}}(\bm{z},\bm{x}))\over\sqrt{V_{J}(\bm{z},\bm{x})}}

\displaystyle={1\over\sqrt{nV_{J}(\bm{z},\bm{x})}}\widetilde{b}_{J}^{(w)}(\bm{z},\bm{x})^{\prime}\widetilde{B}_{J,n}^{(w)^{\prime}}\nu_{n}

\displaystyle\quad+{1\over\sqrt{nV_{J}(\bm{z},\bm{x})}}\widetilde{b}_{J}^{(w)}(\bm{z},\bm{x})^{\prime}\!\left\{(\widetilde{B}_{J,n}^{(w)^{\prime}}\widetilde{B}_{J,n}^{(w)}/n+\varsigma_{J,n}(\check{B}_{J}^{(w)})^{-1})^{-1}\!-I_{J}\right\}\!\widetilde{B}_{J,n}^{(w)^{\prime}}\nu_{n}

\displaystyle=:T_{n,1}+T_{n,2}.

For
T_{n,2}
, observe that

\displaystyle|T_{n,2}|

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{{n\over V_{J}(\bm{z},\bm{x})}}\|\widetilde{b}_{J}^{(w)}(\bm{z},\bm{x})\|\|(\widetilde{B}_{J,n}^{(w)^{\prime}}\widetilde{B}_{J,n}^{(w)}/n+\varsigma_{J,n}(\check{B}_{J}^{(w)})^{-1})^{-1}-I_{J}\|\|\widetilde{B}_{J,n}^{(w)^{\prime}}\nu_{n}/n\|.

(E.3)

From Lemma
F.3
and a similar argument in Steps 1 and 2 of the proof of Proposition
2.1
, we can show that

\displaystyle\|(\widetilde{B}_{J,n}^{(w)^{\prime}}\widetilde{B}_{J,n}^{(w)}/n+\varsigma_{J,n}(\check{B}_{J}^{(w)})^{-1})^{-1}-I_{J}\|

\displaystyle=O_{p}\left(\|(\widetilde{B}_{J,n}^{(w)^{\prime}}\widetilde{B}_{J,n}^{(w)}/n+\varsigma_{J,n}(\check{B}_{J}^{(w)})^{-1})-I_{J}\|\right)

\displaystyle=O_{p}\left(\zeta_{J,n}^{2}\lambda_{J,n}^{2}\sqrt{{\log J\over A_{n}}}\right)=o_{p}((\zeta_{J,n}\lambda_{J,n})^{-1}),

(E.4)

\displaystyle\|\widetilde{B}_{J,n}^{(w)^{\prime}}\nu_{n}/n\|

\displaystyle=O_{p}\left({\zeta_{J,n}\lambda_{J,n}\over\sqrt{n}}\right).

(E.5)

Combining (
E.6
), (
E.3
), (
E.4
), and (
E.5
), we have

\displaystyle|T_{n,2}|

\displaystyle=o_{p}(1).

(E.6)

For
T_{n,1}
, observe that

\displaystyle T_{n,1}={1\over\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}u_{J}\left({\bm{S}_{i}\over A_{n}},\bm{X}(\bm{S}_{i})\right)\varepsilon_{i},

where
u_{J}(\bm{S}_{i}/A_{n},\bm{X}(\bm{S}_{i}))=V_{J}(\bm{z},\bm{x})^{-1/2}\widetilde{b}_{J}^{(w)}(\bm{z},\bm{x})^{\prime}\widetilde{b}_{J}^{(w)}(\bm{S}_{i}/A_{n},\bm{X}(\bm{S}_{i}))\eta(\bm{S}_{i}/A_{n},\bm{X}(\bm{S}_{i}))
.
Note that
|u_{J}(\bm{S}_{i}/A_{n},\bm{X}(\bm{S}_{i}))|\leq C_{\mathfrak{h}}\zeta_{J,n}\lambda_{J,n}
, where
C_{\mathfrak{h}}=\sup_{(\bm{z},\bm{x})\in R_{0}\times\mathbb{R}^{p}}\mathfrak{h}(\bm{z},\bm{x})
. Let
(\Omega,\mathcal{F},\Prob)
denote the probability space that
\{\bm{S}_{i}\}_{i=1}^{n}
,
\{\varepsilon_{i}\}_{i=1}^{n}
, and
\bm{X}=\{\bm{X}(\bm{s}):\bm{s}\in\mathbb{R}^{d}\}
are defined. For
t\geq 1
, let
\mathcal{F}_{t}=\sigma(\{\bm{S}_{i},\varepsilon_{i}\}_{i=1}^{t}\cup\bm{X})
, the
\sigma
-field generated by
\{\bm{S}_{i},\varepsilon_{i}\}_{i=1}^{t}
and
\bm{X}
, and for
t=0
, let
\mathcal{F}_{0}=\sigma(\bm{X})
. Then we can see that
\{u_{J}\left({\bm{S}_{i}\over A_{n}},\bm{X}(\bm{S}_{i})\right)\varepsilon_{i}\}
is a martingale difference sequence with respect to
\{\mathcal{F}_{t}\}
. Therefore, to show (
E.2
), we use a martingale CLT (Corollary 2.8 in
McLeish, 1974
). This requires to verify

(A)

\max_{1\leq i\leq n}|u_{J}(\bm{S}_{i}/A_{n},\bm{X}(\bm{S}_{i}))\varepsilon_{i}|/\sqrt{n}=o_{p}(1)
,

(B)

{1\over n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}u_{J}(\bm{S}_{i}/A_{n},\bm{X}(\bm{S}_{i}))^{2}\varepsilon_{i}^{2}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle p}}{{\to}}1
.

Now we verify Condition (A). Let
\eta>0
be arbitrary. Then we have

\displaystyle\Prob\left(\max_{1\leq i\leq n}\left|u_{J}\left({\bm{S}_{i}\over A_{n}},\bm{X}(\bm{S}_{i})\right)\varepsilon_{i}\right|/\sqrt{n}>\eta\right)

\displaystyle\leq n\Prob\left(\left|u_{J}\left({\bm{S}_{1}\over A_{n}},\bm{X}(\bm{S}_{1})\right)\varepsilon_{1}\right|/\sqrt{n}>\eta\right)

\displaystyle\leq{n\over n\eta^{2}}\E\left[u_{J}\left({\bm{S}_{1}\over A_{n}},\bm{X}(\bm{S}_{1})\right)^{2}\varepsilon_{1}^{2}1\left\{\left|u_{J}\left({\bm{S}_{1}\over A_{n}},\bm{X}(\bm{S}_{1})\right)\varepsilon_{1}\right|/\sqrt{n}>\eta\right\}\right]

\displaystyle\leq{C_{\mathfrak{h}}^{2}\zeta_{J,n}^{2}\lambda_{J,n}^{2}\over\eta}\E\left[\varepsilon_{1}^{2}1\left\{|\varepsilon_{1}|>\eta\sqrt{n}/(C_{\mathfrak{h}}\zeta_{J,n}\lambda_{J,n})\right\}\right]

\displaystyle\leq{(C_{\mathfrak{h}}\zeta_{J,n}\lambda_{J,n})^{q}\over\eta^{q}n^{{q-2\over 2}}}\E\left[|\varepsilon_{1}|^{q}1\left\{|\varepsilon_{1}|>\eta\sqrt{n}/(C_{\mathfrak{h}}\zeta_{J,n}\lambda_{J,n})\right\}\right]=o(1).

Now we verify Condition (B). Applying Lemma
F.4
, we have that

\displaystyle\left|{1\over n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}u_{J}\left({\bm{S}_{i}\over A_{n}},\bm{X}(\bm{S}_{i})\right)^{2}\varepsilon_{i}^{2}-1\right|

\displaystyle=\left|\left({\widetilde{b}_{J}^{(w)}(\bm{z},\bm{x})\over\sqrt{V_{J}(\bm{z},\bm{x})}}\right)^{\prime}(\widehat{\Sigma}-\Sigma)\left({\widetilde{b}_{J}^{(w)}(\bm{z},\bm{x})\over\sqrt{V_{J}(\bm{z},\bm{x})}}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq\left\|{\widetilde{b}_{J}^{(w)}(\bm{z},\bm{x})\over\sqrt{V_{J}(\bm{z},\bm{x})}}\right\|^{2}\|\widehat{\Sigma}-\Sigma\|\lesssim\|\widehat{\Sigma}-\Sigma\|=o_{p}(1).

Therefore, we obtain (
E.2
).

E.7.
Proof of Proposition
B.3

Note that
\widehat{V}(\bm{z}_{1},\bm{x}_{1},\bm{z}_{2},\bm{x}_{2})=\widetilde{b}_{J}^{(w)}(\bm{z}_{1},\bm{x}_{1})^{\prime}\widetilde{H}_{J}\widetilde{b}_{J}^{(w)}(\bm{z}_{2},\bm{x}_{2})
where

\displaystyle\widetilde{H}_{J}

\displaystyle\!=\!\left(\!{\widetilde{B}_{J,n}^{(w)^{\prime}}\widetilde{B}_{J,n}^{(w)}\over n}+\varsigma_{J,n}(\check{B}_{J}^{(w)})^{-1}\!\!\right)^{-1}\left\{{1\over n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\widetilde{b}_{J}^{(w)}\!\!\left({\bm{S}_{i}\over A_{n}},\bm{X}(\bm{S}_{i})\!\right)\widetilde{b}_{J}^{(w)}\!\!\left({\bm{S}_{i}\over A_{n}},\bm{X}(\bm{S}_{i})\!\right)^{\prime}\!\!\right.

\displaystyle\left.\quad\quad\times\left(Y(\bm{S}_{i})-\widehat{\mathfrak{m}}\left({\bm{S}_{i}\over A_{n}},\bm{X}(\bm{S}_{i})\right)\!\right)^{2}\right\}\left(\!{\widetilde{B}_{J,n}^{(w)^{\prime}}\widetilde{B}_{J,n}^{(w)}\over n}+\varsigma_{J,n}(\check{B}_{J}^{(w)})^{-1}\!\!\right)^{-1}.

Then it suffices to show that as
n,J\to\infty
,

\displaystyle\Pi_{n,J}(\bm{z}_{1},\bm{x}_{1},\bm{z}_{2},\bm{x}_{2})

\displaystyle:=\left({\widetilde{b}_{J}^{(w)}(\bm{z}_{1},\bm{x}_{1})\over\|\widetilde{b}_{J}^{(w)}(\bm{z}_{1},\bm{x}_{1})\|}\right)^{\prime}\widetilde{H}_{J}\left({\widetilde{b}_{J}^{(w)}(\bm{z}_{2},\bm{x}_{2})\over\|\widetilde{b}_{J}^{(w)}(\bm{z}_{2},\bm{x}_{2})\|}\right)=\left({\widetilde{b}_{J}^{(w)}(\bm{z}_{1},\bm{x}_{1})\over\|\widetilde{b}_{J}^{(w)}(\bm{z}_{1},\bm{x}_{1})\|}\right)^{\prime}H_{J}\left({\widetilde{b}_{J}^{(w)}(\bm{z}_{2},\bm{x}_{2})\over\|\widetilde{b}_{J}^{(w)}(\bm{z}_{2},\bm{x}_{2})\|}\right)+o_{p}(1).

Now we restrict our attention to the case
(\bm{z}_{1},\bm{x}_{1})=(\bm{z}_{2},\bm{x}_{2})=(\bm{z},\bm{x})
. The proofs for other cases are similar. Define
\Pi_{n,J}(\bm{z},\bm{x}):=\Pi_{n,J}(\bm{z},\bm{x},\bm{z},\bm{x})
. Applying Theorem
B.1
, we have

\displaystyle\Pi_{n,J}(\bm{z},\bm{x})

\displaystyle\!=\!\left({\widetilde{b}_{J}^{(w)}(\bm{z},\bm{x})\over\|\widetilde{b}_{J}^{(w)}(\bm{z},\bm{x})\|}\right)^{\prime}\!\!\!\left({1\over n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\widetilde{b}_{J}^{(w)}\!\!\left({\bm{S}_{i}\over A_{n}},\bm{X}(\bm{S}_{i})\!\right)\!\widetilde{b}_{J}^{(w)}\!\!\left({\bm{S}_{i}\over A_{n}},\bm{X}(\bm{S}_{i})\!\right)^{\prime}\!\!\varepsilon_{i}^{2}\right)\!\!\left({\widetilde{b}_{J}^{(w)}(\bm{z},\bm{x})\over\|\widetilde{b}_{J}^{(w)}(\bm{z},\bm{x})\|}\right)^{\prime}\!\!+o_{p}(1).

Then Lemma
F.4
yields the desired result.

Appendix F
Auxiliary lemmas

Lemma F.1

(
Tropp, 2012
)
.

Let
\{\Theta_{n,i}\}_{i=1}^{n}
be a finite sequence of independent random matrices with dimensions
d_{1}\times d_{2}
. Assume
\E[\Theta_{n,i}]=0
for each
i
and
\max_{1\leq i\leq n}\|\Theta_{n,i}\|\leq M_{n}
, and define

\sigma_{n}^{2}=\max\left\{\left\|\sum_{i=1}^{n}\E[\Theta_{n,i}\Theta^{\prime}_{n,i}]\right\|,\left\|\sum_{i=1}^{n}\E[\Theta^{\prime}_{n,i}\Theta_{n,i}]\right\|\right\}.

Then for all
t\geq 0
,

\Prob\left(\left\|\sum_{i=1}^{n}\Theta_{n,i}\right\|\geq t\right)\leq(d_{1}+d_{2})\exp\left({-t^{2}/2\over\sigma_{n}^{2}+M_{n}t/3}\right).

Corollary F.1
.

Under the conditions of Lemma
F.1
, if
M_{n}\sqrt{\log(d_{1}+d_{2})}=o(\sigma_{n})
then

\left\|\sum_{i=1}^{n}\Theta_{n,i}\right\|=O_{p}\left(\sigma_{n}\sqrt{\log(d_{1}+d_{2})}\right).

Proof.

Letting
t=C\sigma_{n}\sqrt{\log(d_{1}+d_{2})}
for sufficiently large
C>1
, we have

\displaystyle\Prob\left(\left\|\sum_{i=1}^{n}\Theta_{n,i}\right\|\geq C\sigma_{n}\sqrt{\log(d_{1}+d_{2})}\right)

\displaystyle\leq(d_{1}+d_{2})\exp\left({-C^{2}\sigma_{n}^{2}\log(d_{1}+d_{2})\over\sigma_{n}^{2}+CM_{n}\sigma_{n}\sqrt{(d_{1}+d_{2})}/3}\right)

\displaystyle=(d_{1}+d_{2})\exp\left({-C^{2}\log(d_{1}+d_{2})\over 1+CM_{n}\sqrt{(d_{1}+d_{2})}/(3\sigma_{n})}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim(d_{1}+d_{2})\exp\left(-\log(d_{1}+d_{2})^{C^{2}}\right)=(d_{1}+d_{2})^{1-C^{2}},

which yields the desired result.
∎

Lemma F.2
.

Under Assumptions
2.1
(ii) and
2.3
(iii), we have

\|\widetilde{\Psi}^{\prime}_{J,n}\widetilde{\Psi}_{J,n}/n-I_{J}\|=O_{p}\left(\zeta_{J}\lambda_{J}\sqrt{\log J\over n}\right).

Proof.

Recall
\check{\Psi}_{J}=\E\left[\psi_{J}(\bm{S}_{i}/A_{n})\psi_{J}(\bm{S}_{i}/A_{n})^{\prime}\right]
. Note that
\E[\widetilde{\psi}_{J}(\bm{S}_{i}/A_{n})\widetilde{\psi}_{J}(\bm{S}_{i}/A_{n})^{\prime}]=I_{J}
and

\displaystyle\widetilde{\Psi}^{\prime}_{J,n}\widetilde{\Psi}_{J,n}

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=1}^{n}\widetilde{\psi}_{J}\left({\bm{S}_{i}\over A_{n}}\right)\widetilde{\psi}_{J}\left({\bm{S}_{i}\over A_{n}}\right)^{\prime}=\sum_{i=1}^{n}\check{\Psi}_{J}^{-1/2}\psi_{J}\left({\bm{S}_{i}\over A_{n}}\right)\psi_{J}\left({\bm{S}_{i}\over A_{n}}\right)^{\prime}\check{\Psi}_{J}^{-1/2}.

Moreover,

\displaystyle\left\|\widetilde{\psi}_{J}\left({\bm{S}_{i}\over A_{n}}\right)\widetilde{\psi}_{J}\left({\bm{S}_{i}\over A_{n}}\right)^{\prime}-I_{J}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\widetilde{\psi}_{J}\left({\bm{S}_{i}\over A_{n}}\right)\widetilde{\psi}_{J}\left({\bm{S}_{i}\over A_{n}}\right)^{\prime}\right\|+\|I_{J}\|\leq\left\|\check{\Psi}_{J}^{-1/2}\psi_{J}\left({\bm{S}_{i}\over A_{n}}\right)\psi_{J}\left({\bm{S}_{i}\over A_{n}}\right)^{\prime}\check{\Psi}_{J}^{-1/2}\right\|+1

\displaystyle\leq\|\check{\Psi}_{J}^{-1/2}\|^{2}\left\|\psi_{J}\left({\bm{S}_{i}\over A_{n}}\right)\psi_{J}\left({\bm{S}_{i}\over A_{n}}\right)^{\prime}\right\|+1\leq\zeta_{J}^{2}\lambda_{J}^{2}+1.

Further,

\displaystyle\E\left[\left(\widetilde{\psi}_{J}\left({\bm{S}_{i}\over A_{n}}\right)\widetilde{\psi}_{J}\left({\bm{S}_{i}\over A_{n}}\right)^{\prime}-I_{J}\right)\left(\widetilde{\psi}_{J}\left({\bm{S}_{i}\over A_{n}}\right)\widetilde{\psi}_{J}\left({\bm{S}_{i}\over A_{n}}\right)^{\prime}-I_{J}\right)^{\prime}\right]

\displaystyle=\E\left[\widetilde{\psi}_{J}\left({\bm{S}_{i}\over A_{n}}\right)\widetilde{\psi}_{J}\left({\bm{S}_{i}\over A_{n}}\right)^{\prime}\widetilde{\psi}_{J}\left({\bm{S}_{i}\over A_{n}}\right)\widetilde{\psi}_{J}\left({\bm{S}_{i}\over A_{n}}\right)^{\prime}\right]-2\E\left[\widetilde{\psi}_{J}\left({\bm{S}_{i}\over A_{n}}\right)\widetilde{\psi}_{J}\left({\bm{S}_{i}\over A_{n}}\right)^{\prime}\right]+I_{J}

\displaystyle=\E\left[\left\|\widetilde{\psi}_{J}\left({\bm{S}_{i}\over A_{n}}\right)\right\|^{2}\widetilde{\psi}_{J}\left({\bm{S}_{i}\over A_{n}}\right)\widetilde{\psi}_{J}\left({\bm{S}_{i}\over A_{n}}\right)^{\prime}\right]-I_{J}.

Then we have

\displaystyle\left\|\E\left[\left(\widetilde{\psi}_{J}\left({\bm{S}_{i}\over A_{n}}\right)\widetilde{\psi}_{J}\left({\bm{S}_{i}\over A_{n}}\right)^{\prime}-I_{J}\right)\left(\widetilde{\psi}_{J}\left({\bm{S}_{i}\over A_{n}}\right)\widetilde{\psi}_{J}\left({\bm{S}_{i}\over A_{n}}\right)^{\prime}-I_{J}\right)^{\prime}\right]\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\E\left[\left\|\widetilde{\psi}_{J}\left({\bm{S}_{i}\over A_{n}}\right)\right\|^{2}\widetilde{\psi}_{J}\left({\bm{S}_{i}\over A_{n}}\right)\widetilde{\psi}_{J}\left({\bm{S}_{i}\over A_{n}}\right)^{\prime}\right]\right\|+\|I_{J}\|

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{\bm{z}\in R_{0}}\|\widetilde{\psi}_{J}(\bm{z})\|^{2}\left\|\E\left[\widetilde{\psi}_{J}\left({\bm{S}_{i}\over A_{n}}\right)\widetilde{\psi}_{J}\left({\bm{S}_{i}\over A_{n}}\right)^{\prime}\right]\right\|+1\leq\zeta_{J}^{2}\lambda_{J}^{2}+1.

Therefore, applying Corollary
F.1
with
M_{n}=(\zeta_{J}^{2}\lambda_{J}^{2}+1)/n
and
\sigma_{n}^{2}=(\zeta_{J}^{2}\lambda_{J}^{2}+1)/n
, we obtain the desired result.
∎

Lemma F.3
.

Suppose that Assumptions
2.1
(ii),
B.1
(i), (ii), and
B.3
(iii) hold. Assume that
\bm{X}=\{\bm{X}(\bm{s}):\bm{s}\in\mathbb{R}^{d}\}
and
\{\bm{S}_{i}\}_{i=1}^{n}
are mutually independent. Then

\|\widetilde{B}_{J,n}^{(w)^{\prime}}\widetilde{B}_{J,n}^{(w)}/n-I_{J}\|=O_{p}\left(\zeta_{J,n}^{2}\lambda_{J,n}^{2}\sqrt{\log J\over A_{n}}\right).

Proof.

Define
\check{B}_{J}^{(w)}=\E[b_{J}^{(w)}(\bm{S}_{i}/A_{n},\bm{X}(\bm{S}_{1}))b_{J}^{(w)}(\bm{S}_{i}/A_{n},\bm{X}(\bm{S}_{1}))^{\prime}]
. Note that

\displaystyle\widetilde{B}_{J,n}^{(w)^{\prime}}\widetilde{B}_{J,n}^{(w)}

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=1}^{n}\widetilde{b}_{J}^{(w)}\left({\bm{S}_{i}\over A_{n}},\bm{X}(\bm{S}_{i})\right)\widetilde{b}_{J}^{(w)}\left({\bm{S}_{i}\over A_{n}},\bm{X}(\bm{S}_{i})\right)^{\prime}

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=1}^{n}(\check{B}_{J}^{(w)})^{-1/2}b_{J}^{(w)}\left({\bm{S}_{i}\over A_{n}},\bm{X}(\bm{S}_{i})\right)b_{J}^{(w)}\left({\bm{S}_{i}\over A_{n}},\bm{X}(\bm{S}_{i})\right)^{\prime}(\check{B}_{J}^{(w)})^{-1/2}

and
\E[\widetilde{b}_{J}^{(w)}\left(\bm{S}_{i}/A_{n},\bm{X}(\bm{S}_{1})\right)\widetilde{b}_{J}^{(w)}\left(\bm{S}_{i}/A_{n},\bm{X}(\bm{S}_{1})\right)^{\prime}]=I_{J}
. Observe that

\displaystyle\left\|\widetilde{b}_{J}^{(w)}\left({\bm{S}_{i}\over A_{n}},\bm{X}(\bm{S}_{i})\right)\widetilde{b}_{J}^{(w)}\left({\bm{S}_{i}\over A_{n}},\bm{X}(\bm{S}_{i})\right)^{\prime}-I_{J}\right\|\lesssim\zeta_{J,n}^{2}\lambda_{J,n}^{2}+1.

Further, we have

\displaystyle\widetilde{B}_{J,n}^{(w)^{\prime}}\widetilde{B}_{J,n}^{(w)}/n-I_{J}

\displaystyle={1\over n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\widetilde{b}_{J}^{(w)}\left({\bm{S}_{i}\over A_{n}},\bm{X}(\bm{S}_{i})\right)\widetilde{b}_{J}^{(w)}\left({\bm{S}_{i}\over A_{n}},\bm{X}(\bm{S}_{i})\right)^{\prime}-I_{J}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\bm{\ell}\in L_{n1}}\!\!\!\!b_{1}^{(\bm{\ell};\bm{\Delta}_{0})}\!+\!\sum_{\bm{\Delta}\neq\bm{\Delta}_{0}}\sum_{\bm{\ell}\in L_{n1}}\!\!\!\!b_{1}^{(\bm{\ell};\bm{\Delta})}\!+\!\sum_{\bm{\Delta}\in\{1,2\}^{d}}\sum_{\bm{\ell}\in L_{n2}}\!\!\!\!b_{1}^{(\bm{\ell};\bm{\Delta})},

where

b_{1}^{(\bm{\ell};\bm{\Delta})}={1\over n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left\{\widetilde{b}_{J}^{(w)}\left({\bm{S}_{i}\over A_{n}},\bm{X}(\bm{S}_{i})\right)\widetilde{b}_{J}^{(w)}\left({\bm{S}_{i}\over A_{n}},\bm{X}(\bm{S}_{i})\right)^{\prime}-I_{J}\right\}1\{\bm{S}_{i}\in\Gamma_{n}(\bm{\ell};\bm{\Delta})\cap R_{n}\}.

For
\bm{\Delta}\in\{1,2\}^{d}
, let
\{\widetilde{b}_{1}^{(\bm{\ell};\bm{\Delta})}\}_{\bm{\ell}\in L_{n1}\cup L_{n2}}
be independent random variables such that
b_{1}^{(\bm{\ell};\bm{\Delta})}
and
\widetilde{b}_{1}^{(\bm{\ell};\bm{\Delta})}
have the same distribution. Applying Lemma
F.7
below with
M_{h}=1
,
m\sim A_{n}(A_{n}^{(1)})^{-1}
, and
\tau\sim\beta(\underline{A}_{n2};A_{n})
, we have that for
\bm{\Delta}\in\{1,2\}^{d}
,

\displaystyle\sup_{t>0}\left|\Prob\left(\left|\sum_{\bm{\ell}\in L_{n1}}b_{1}^{(\bm{\ell};\bm{\Delta})}\right|>t\right)-\Prob\left(\left|\sum_{\bm{\ell}\in L_{n1}}\widetilde{b}_{1}^{(\bm{\ell};\bm{\Delta})}\right|>t\right)\right|\lesssim\left({A_{n}\over A_{n}^{(1)}}\right)\beta(\underline{A}_{n2};A_{n})=o(1),

(F.1)

\displaystyle\sup_{t>0}\left|\Prob\left(\left|\sum_{\bm{\ell}\in L_{n2}}b_{1}^{(\bm{\ell};\bm{\Delta})}\right|>t\right)-\Prob\left(\left|\sum_{\bm{\ell}\in L_{n2}}\widetilde{b}_{1}^{(\bm{\ell};\bm{\Delta})}\right|>t\right)\right|\lesssim\left({A_{n}\over A_{n}^{(1)}}\right)\beta(\underline{A}_{n2};A_{n})=o(1).

(F.2)

From (
F.1
) and (
F.2
), we have

\displaystyle\Prob\left(\left\|\widetilde{B}_{J,n}^{(w)^{\prime}}\widetilde{B}_{J,n}^{(w)}/n-I_{J}\right\|>C2^{d+2}\zeta_{J,n}^{2}\lambda_{J,n}^{2}\sqrt{{\log n\over A_{n}}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{\bm{\Delta}\in\{1,2\}^{d}}\widehat{\mathcal{Q}}_{n1}(\bm{\Delta})+\sum_{\bm{\Delta}\in\{1,2\}^{d}}\widehat{\mathcal{Q}}_{n2}(\bm{\Delta})+2^{d+1}\underbrace{\left({A_{n}\over A_{n}^{(1)}}\right)\beta(\underline{A}_{n2};A_{n})}_{=o(1)},

where

\displaystyle\widehat{\mathcal{Q}}_{nj}(\bm{\Delta})

\displaystyle=\Prob\left(\left\|\sum_{\bm{\ell}\in L_{nj}}\widetilde{b}_{1}^{(\bm{\ell};\bm{\Delta})}\right\|>Cn\zeta_{J,n}^{2}\lambda_{J,n}^{2}\sqrt{{\log n\over A_{n}}}\right),\ j=1,2.

Now we restrict our attention to show that
\widehat{\mathcal{Q}}_{n1}(\bm{\Delta})=o(1)
for
\bm{\Delta}\neq\bm{\Delta}_{0}
. The proofs for other cases are similar. Note that

\displaystyle\left\|\widetilde{b}_{1}^{(\bm{\ell};\bm{\Delta})}\right\|

\displaystyle\lesssim A_{n}^{-1}A_{n}^{(1)}(\zeta_{J,n}^{2}\lambda_{J,n}^{2}+1)\ a.s.\ (\text{from Lemma \ref{lem:number-summands}}).

Further, observe that

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{\bm{\ell}\in L_{n1}}\E\left[\widetilde{b}_{1}^{(\bm{\ell};\bm{\Delta})}\widetilde{b}_{1}^{(\bm{\ell};\bm{\Delta})^{\prime}}\right]\right\|

\displaystyle\lesssim{n\over n^{2}}\E\left[\left\|\left\{\widetilde{b}_{J}^{(w)}\left({\bm{S}_{1}\over A_{n}},\bm{X}(\bm{S}_{1})\right)\widetilde{b}_{J}^{(w)}\left({\bm{S}_{1}\over A_{n}},\bm{X}(\bm{S}_{1})\right)^{\prime}-I_{J}\right\}\right.\right.

\displaystyle\left.\left.\quad\times\left\{\widetilde{b}_{J}^{(w)}\left({\bm{S}_{1}\over A_{n}},\bm{X}(\bm{S}_{1})\right)\widetilde{b}_{J}^{(w)}\left({\bm{S}_{1}\over A_{n}},\bm{X}(\bm{S}_{1})\right)^{\prime}-I_{J}\right\}\right\|\right]

\displaystyle\quad+{n(n-1)\over n^{2}}\E\left[\left\|\left\{\widetilde{b}_{J}^{(w)}\left({\bm{S}_{1}\over A_{n}},\bm{X}(\bm{S}_{1})\right)\widetilde{b}_{J}^{(w)}\left({\bm{S}_{1}\over A_{n}},\bm{X}(\bm{S}_{1})\right)^{\prime}-I_{J}\right\}\right.\right.

\displaystyle\left.\left.\quad\quad\times\left\{\widetilde{b}_{J}^{(w)}\left({\bm{S}_{2}\over A_{n}},\bm{X}(\bm{S}_{2})\right)\widetilde{b}_{J}^{(w)}\left({\bm{S}_{2}\over A_{n}},\bm{X}(\bm{S}_{2})\right)^{\prime}-I_{J}\right\}^{\prime}\right\|\right]=:W_{n,1}+W_{n,2}.

For
W_{n,1}
, we have
W_{n,1}\lesssim(\zeta_{J,n}^{4}\lambda_{J,n}^{4}+1)/n
. For
W_{n,2}
, letting
\bm{Z}_{i}=A_{n}\bm{S}_{i}
,
\bm{W}=\bm{S}_{1}-\bm{S}_{2}
, we have

\displaystyle W_{n,2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\E\left[\left\|\left\{\widetilde{b}_{J}^{(w)}\left({\bm{S}_{1}\over A_{n}},\bm{X}(\bm{0})\right)\widetilde{b}_{J}^{(w)}\left({\bm{S}_{1}\over A_{n}},\bm{X}(\bm{0})\right)^{\prime}-I_{J}\right\}\right.\right.

\displaystyle\left.\left.\quad\quad\times\left\{\widetilde{b}_{J}^{(w)}\left({\bm{S}_{2}\over A_{n}},\bm{X}(\bm{S}_{1}-\bm{S}_{2})\right)\widetilde{b}_{J}^{(w)}\left({\bm{S}_{2}\over A_{n}},\bm{X}(\bm{S}_{1}-\bm{S}_{2})\right)^{\prime}-I_{J}\right\}^{\prime}\right\|\right]

\displaystyle={1\over A_{n}}\E\left[\left\|\left\{\widetilde{b}_{J}^{(w)}\left({\bm{W}\over A_{n}}+\bm{Z}_{2},\bm{X}(\bm{0})\right)\widetilde{b}_{J}^{(w)}\left({\bm{W}\over A_{n}}+\bm{Z}_{2},\bm{X}(\bm{0})\right)^{\prime}-I_{J}\right\}\right.\right.

\displaystyle\left.\left.\quad\quad\times\left\{\widetilde{b}_{J}^{(w)}\left(\bm{Z}_{2},\bm{X}(\bm{W})\right)\widetilde{b}_{J}^{(w)}\left(\bm{Z}_{2},\bm{X}(\bm{W})\right)^{\prime}-I_{J}\right\}^{\prime}\right\|\right]\lesssim{\zeta_{J,n}^{4}\lambda_{J,n}^{4}\over A_{n}}.

Then applying Corollary
F.1
with
M_{n}=C_{1}A_{n}^{-1}A_{n}^{(1)}(\zeta_{J,n}^{2}\lambda_{J,n}^{2}+1)
and
\sigma_{n}^{2}=C_{2}A_{n}^{-1}\zeta_{J,n}^{4}\lambda_{J,n}^{4}
for some constants
C_{1},C_{2}>0
independent of
n
, we obtain the desired result.
∎

Define

\displaystyle\Sigma

\displaystyle=\E\left[\widetilde{b}_{J}^{(w)}\left({\bm{S}_{1}\over A_{n}},\bm{X}(\bm{S}_{1})\right)\widetilde{b}_{J}^{(w)}\left({\bm{S}_{1}\over A_{n}},\bm{X}(\bm{S}_{1})\right)^{\prime}\mathfrak{h}\left({\bm{S}_{1}\over A_{n}},\bm{X}(\bm{S}_{1})\right)^{2}\varepsilon_{1}^{2}\right],

\displaystyle\widehat{\Sigma}

\displaystyle={1\over n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\widetilde{b}_{J}^{(w)}\left({\bm{S}_{i}\over A_{n}},\bm{X}(\bm{S}_{i})\right)\widetilde{b}_{J}^{(w)}\left({\bm{S}_{i}\over A_{n}},\bm{X}(\bm{S}_{i})\right)^{\prime}\mathfrak{h}\left({\bm{S}_{i}\over A_{n}},\bm{X}(\bm{S}_{i})\right)^{2}\varepsilon_{i}^{2}.

Lemma F.4
.

Suppose that Assumptions
2.1
(i), (ii),
B.1
,
B.3
(iii) hold.

Additionally, assume
(\zeta_{J,n}\lambda_{J,n})^{{2q\over q-2}}\lesssim\sqrt{{A_{n}\over(\log n)^{2}}}
. Then
\|\widehat{\Sigma}-\Sigma\|=o_{p}(1)
.

Proof.

Define
\widehat{\Sigma}_{j}={1\over n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}(\Xi_{j,i}-\E[\Xi_{j,i}])
,
j=1,2
,
where

\displaystyle\Xi_{1,i}

\displaystyle=\widetilde{b}_{J}^{(w)}\left({\bm{S}_{i}\over A_{n}},\bm{X}(\bm{S}_{i})\right)\widetilde{b}_{J}^{(w)}\left({\bm{S}_{i}\over A_{n}},\bm{X}(\bm{S}_{i})\right)^{\prime}\mathfrak{h}\left({\bm{S}_{i}\over A_{n}},\bm{X}(\bm{S}_{i})\right)^{2}\varepsilon_{i}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad\times 1\left\{\left\|\widetilde{b}_{J}^{(w)}\left({\bm{S}_{i}\over A_{n}},\bm{X}(\bm{S}_{i})\right)\widetilde{b}_{J}^{(w)}\left({\bm{S}_{i}\over A_{n}},\bm{X}(\bm{S}_{i})\right)^{\prime}\varepsilon_{i}^{2}\right\|\leq L_{n}^{2}\right\},

\displaystyle\Xi_{2,i}

\displaystyle=\widetilde{b}_{J}^{(w)}\left({\bm{S}_{i}\over A_{n}},\bm{X}(\bm{S}_{i})\right)\widetilde{b}_{J}^{(w)}\left({\bm{S}_{i}\over A_{n}},\bm{X}(\bm{S}_{i})\right)^{\prime}\mathfrak{h}\left({\bm{S}_{i}\over A_{n}},\bm{X}(\bm{S}_{i})\right)^{2}\varepsilon_{i}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad\times 1\left\{\left\|\widetilde{b}_{J}^{(w)}\left({\bm{S}_{i}\over A_{n}},\bm{X}(\bm{S}_{i})\right)\widetilde{b}_{J}^{(w)}\left({\bm{S}_{i}\over A_{n}},\bm{X}(\bm{S}_{i})\right)^{\prime}\varepsilon_{i}^{2}\right\|>L_{n}^{2}\right\}

and where
L_{n}=(\zeta_{J,n}\lambda_{J,n})^{{q\over q-2}}
. Note that
\widehat{\Sigma}-\Sigma=\widehat{\Sigma}_{1}+\widehat{\Sigma}_{2}
. Then it is sufficient to show
\|\widehat{\Sigma}_{j}\|=o_{p}(1)
,
j=1,2
.

First, we show
\|\widehat{\Sigma}_{1}\|=o_{p}(1)
. Observe that
\|\Xi_{1,i}\|\leq C_{\mathfrak{h}}^{2}L_{n}^{2}
where
C_{\mathfrak{h}}=\sup_{(\bm{z},\bm{x})\in R_{0}\times\mathbb{R}^{p}}\mathfrak{h}(\bm{z},\bm{x})
. Then we have
\|\Xi_{1,i}-\E[\Xi_{1,i}]\|\leq 2C_{\mathfrak{h}}^{2}L_{n}^{2}
. Decompose

\displaystyle\widehat{\Sigma}_{1}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\bm{\ell}\in L_{n1}}\!\!\Xi_{1}^{(\bm{\ell};\bm{\Delta}_{0})}\!+\!\sum_{\bm{\Delta}\neq\bm{\Delta}_{0}}\sum_{\bm{\ell}\in L_{n1}}\!\!\Xi_{1}^{(\bm{\ell};\bm{\Delta})}\!+\!\sum_{\bm{\Delta}\in\{1,2\}^{d}}\sum_{\bm{\ell}\in L_{n2}}\!\!\Xi_{1}^{(\bm{\ell};\bm{\Delta})},

where
\Xi_{1}^{(\bm{\ell};\bm{\Delta})}={1\over n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left\{\Xi_{1,i}-\E[\Xi_{1,i}]\right\}1\{\bm{S}_{i}\in\Gamma_{n}(\bm{\ell};\bm{\Delta})\cap R_{n}\}
. Observe that

\displaystyle\|\Xi_{1}^{(\bm{\ell};\bm{\Delta})}\|

\displaystyle\leq{1\over n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\|\Xi_{1,i}-\E[\Xi_{1,i}]\|1\{\bm{S}_{i}\in\Gamma_{n}(\bm{\ell};\bm{\Delta})\}

\displaystyle\leq{2C_{\mathfrak{h}}^{2}L_{n}^{2}\over n}nA_{n}^{-1}A_{n}^{(1)}={2C_{\mathfrak{h}}^{2}A_{n}^{(1)}L_{n}^{2}\over A_{n}}\ a.s.\ (\text{from Lemma \ref{lem:number-summands}})

(F.3)

and

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{\bm{\ell}\in L_{n1}}\E\left[\Xi_{1}^{(\bm{\ell};\bm{\Delta})}\Xi_{1}^{(\bm{\ell};\bm{\Delta})^{\prime}}\right]\right\|

\displaystyle\leq{n\over n^{2}}\left\|\E\left[\left(\Xi_{1,1}-\E[\Xi_{1,1}]\right)\left(\Xi_{1,1}-\E[\Xi_{1,1}]\right)^{\prime}1\{\bm{S}_{1}\in\cup_{\bm{\ell}\in L_{n1}}\Gamma_{n}(\bm{\ell};\bm{\Delta})\}\right]\right\|

\displaystyle\quad+{n(n-1)\over n^{2}}\left\|\E\left[\left(\Xi_{1,1}-\E[\Xi_{1,1}]\right)\left(\Xi_{1,2}-\E[\Xi_{1,2}]\right)^{\prime}1\{\bm{S}_{1},\bm{S}_{2}\in\cup_{\bm{\ell}\in L_{n1}}\Gamma_{n}(\bm{\ell};\bm{\Delta})\}\right]\right\|

\displaystyle\leq{n\over n^{2}}\E\left[\left\|\left(\Xi_{1,1}-\E[\Xi_{1,1}]\right)\left(\Xi_{1,1}-\E[\Xi_{1,1}]\right)^{\prime}\right\|\right]+{n(n-1)\over n^{2}}\E\left[\left\|\left(\Xi_{1,1}-\E[\Xi_{1,1}]\right)\left(\Xi_{1,2}-\E[\Xi_{1,2}]\right)^{\prime}\right\|\right]

\displaystyle\leq{4C_{\mathfrak{h}}^{4}L_{n}^{4}\over n}+{4C_{\mathfrak{h}}^{4}L_{n}^{4}\over A_{n}}\lesssim{L_{n}^{4}\over A_{n}}.

(F.4)

Therefore, applying Lemma
F.1
and a similar argument to the proof of Lemma
F.3
with (
F
) and (
F
), we have
\|\widehat{\Sigma}_{1}\|=O_{p}\left({L_{n}^{2}\sqrt{\log J\over A_{n}}}\right)=o_{p}(1)
.

Next we show
\|\widehat{\Sigma}_{2}\|=o_{p}(1)
. Observe that

\displaystyle\|\Xi_{2,i}\|

\displaystyle\leq C_{\mathfrak{h}}^{2}\left\|\widetilde{b}_{J}^{(w)}\left({\bm{S}_{i}\over A_{n}},\bm{X}(\bm{S}_{i})\right)\widetilde{b}_{J}^{(w)}\left({\bm{S}_{i}\over A_{n}},\bm{X}(\bm{S}_{i})\right)^{\prime}\varepsilon_{i}^{2}\right\|

\displaystyle\quad\times 1\left\{\left\|\widetilde{b}_{J}^{(w)}\left({\bm{S}_{i}\over A_{n}},\bm{X}(\bm{S}_{i})\right)\widetilde{b}_{J}^{(w)}\left({\bm{S}_{i}\over A_{n}},\bm{X}(\bm{S}_{i})\right)^{\prime}\varepsilon_{i}^{2}\right\|>L_{n}^{2}\right\}

\displaystyle\leq C_{\mathfrak{h}}^{2}\zeta_{J,n}^{2}\lambda_{J,n}^{2}|\varepsilon_{i}|^{2}1\{\zeta_{J,n}^{2}\lambda_{J,n}^{2}|\varepsilon_{i}|^{2}>L_{n}^{2}\}.

Then we have

\displaystyle\E[\|\widehat{\Sigma}_{2}\|]

\displaystyle\leq 2\E[\|\Xi_{2,i}\|]\leq 2C_{\mathfrak{h}}^{2}\zeta_{J,n}^{2}\lambda_{J,n}^{2}\E[|\varepsilon_{i}|^{2}1\{\zeta_{J,n}^{2}\lambda_{J,n}^{2}|\varepsilon_{i}|^{2}>L_{n}^{2}\}]

\displaystyle\leq{2C_{\mathfrak{h}}^{2}(\zeta_{J,n}\lambda_{J,n})^{2}(\zeta_{J,n}\lambda_{J,n})^{q-2}\over L_{n}^{q-2}}\E[|\varepsilon_{1}|^{q}1\{|\varepsilon_{i}|^{2}>L_{n}^{2}/(\zeta_{J,n}\lambda_{J,n})^{2}\}]=o(1).

Hence, applying Markov’s inequality, we have
\|\widehat{\Sigma}_{2}\|=o_{p}(1)
.
∎

Lemma F.5
.

Let
B
and
C
be
J\times J
matrices. Assume that
A=B+C
,
B
, and
C
are non-singular, and
\lambda_{\text{min}}(C^{-1})>\lambda_{\text{max}}(C^{-1})\{\|A-I_{J}\|(1+\|B-I_{J}\|)+\|B-I_{J}\|\}
. Then

\displaystyle\|A^{-1}-B^{-1}\|

\displaystyle\leq{1\over\lambda_{\text{min}}(C^{-1})-\lambda_{\text{max}}(C^{-1})\{\|A-I_{J}\|(1+\|B-I_{J}\|)+\|B-I_{J}\|\}}.

Proof.

Observe that

\displaystyle\|A^{-1}-B^{-1}\|

\displaystyle=\sup_{\|x\|\neq 0}{\|(A^{-1}-B^{-1})\bm{x}\|\over\|\bm{x}\|}=\sup_{\|A\bm{x}\|\neq 0}{\|(I_{J}-B^{-1}A)\bm{x}\|\over\|A\bm{x}\|}

\displaystyle=\sup_{\|A\bm{x}\|\neq 0}{\|B^{-1}C\bm{x}\|\over\|A\bm{x}\|}=\sup_{\|AC^{-1}B\bm{y}\|\neq 0}{\|\bm{y}\|\over\|AC^{-1}B\bm{y}\|}={1\over\inf_{\|x\|=1}\|AC^{-1}B\bm{x}\|}.

(F.5)

Note that

\displaystyle\|AC^{-1}B\bm{x}\|

\displaystyle\geq\|C^{-1}B\bm{x}\|-\|(A-I_{J})C^{-1}B\bm{x}\|

\displaystyle\geq\|C^{-1}\bm{x}\|-\|C^{-1}(B-I_{J})\bm{x}\|-\|(A-I_{J})C^{-1}B\bm{x}\|

\displaystyle\geq\{\lambda_{\text{min}}(C^{-1})-\|C^{-1}\|\left(\|A-I_{J}\|(1+\|B-I_{J}\|)+\|B-I_{J}\|\right)\}\|\bm{x}\|

\displaystyle\geq\{\lambda_{\text{min}}(C^{-1})-\lambda_{\text{max}}(C^{-1})\left(\|A-I_{J}\|(1+\|B-I_{J}\|)+\|B-I_{J}\|\right)\}\|\bm{x}\|.

(F.6)

Combining (
F
) and (
F
), we obtain the desired result.
∎

We refer to the following lemmas without those proofs.

Lemma F.6

((5.19) in
Lahiri, 2003b
)
.

Under Assumption 2.2, we have

\Prob\left(\sum_{i=1}^{n}1\{\bm{X}_{i}\in\Gamma_{n}(\bm{\ell};\bm{\Delta})\}>C|\Gamma_{n}(\bm{\ell};\bm{\Delta})|nA_{n}^{-1}\ \text{for some $\bm{\ell}\in L_{n1}$, i.o.}\right)=0

for any
\bm{\Delta}\in\{1,2\}^{d}
, where
C>0
is a sufficiently large constant.

Lemma F.7

(Corollary 2.7 in
Yu, 1994
)
.

Let
m\in\mathbb{N}
and let
Q
be a probability measure on a product space
(\prod_{i=1}^{m}\Omega_{i},\prod_{i=1}^{m}\Sigma_{i})
with marginal measures
Q_{i}
on
(\Omega_{i},\Sigma_{i})
. Suppose that
h
is a bounded measurable function on the product probability space such that
|h|\leq M_{h}<\infty
. For
1\leq a\leq b\leq m
, let
Q_{a}^{b}
be the marginal measure on
(\prod_{i=a}^{b}\Omega_{i},\prod_{i=a}^{b}\Sigma_{i})
. For a given
\tau>0
, suppose that, for all
1\leq k\leq m-1
,

\displaystyle\|Q-Q_{1}^{k}\times Q_{k+1}^{m}\|_{TV}\leq 2\tau,

(F.7)

where
Q_{1}^{k}\times Q_{k+1}^{m}
is a product measure and
\|\cdot\|_{TV}
is the total variation. Then
|Qh-Ph|\leq 2M_{h}(m-1)\tau
where
P=\prod_{i=1}^{m}Q_{i}
,
Qh=\int hdQ
, and
Ph=\int hdP
.

Lemma F.8

(Bernstein’s inequality)
.

Let
X_{1},\ldots,X_{n}
be independent zero-mean random variables. Suppose that
\max_{1\leq i\leq n}|X_{i}|\leq M<\infty
a.s. Then, for all
t>0
,

\displaystyle\Prob\left(\sum_{i=1}^{n}X_{i}\geq t\right)\leq\exp\left(-{{t^{2}\over 2}\over\sum_{i=1}^{n}\E[X_{i}^{2}]+{Mt\over 3}}\right).
